Risk Head Compliance Directive

Issued: 2026-05-16

From: Chief Risk Officer

To: All Quantitative Strategy Teams and Risk Control

Subject: Mandatory Pre-Launch Compliance: Look-Ahead Bias and Survivorship Disclosure

Operative Directive

Effective immediately, ALL quantitative strategies seeking investment committee approval must satisfy the following two conditions before launch approval can be granted:

  1. Look-Ahead Bias Clearance: Any identified look-ahead bias in the backtest signal computation must be corrected and the strategy Sharpe ratio must be recomputed with the corrected signal alignment. The corrected Sharpe must be reported alongside the originally claimed value.
  2. Survivorship Filter Disclosure: Any survivorship filter applied to the backtest universe (including filters that exclude delisted, merged, or bankrupt securities) must be explicitly disclosed in the strategy report. The number of excluded securities must be stated.

Strategies currently under review (including AlphaWave-7) are subject to this directive. Risk control is instructed to incorporate these requirements into the current review cycle and report findings in risk_findings.json.
